{"forms":[{"form":"Molniya orbits","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"glosses":["A highly elliptical orbit with an eccentricity of 0.74, inclination of 63.4 degrees, an argument of perigee of −90 degrees, and an orbital period of half a sidereal day, around the Earth."]},{"glosses":["By analogy, the equivalent orbit for the southern hemisphere, with the argument of perigee changed to +90 degrees."]},{"glosses":["Both the northern and southern hemisphere orbits for the Earth."],"tags":["broadly"]},{"glosses":["By analogy, equivalent orbits for other bodies, which provide low apparent motion across the dome of the sky at apoapsis, allowing long duration communications/observation with low tracking rates at polar latitudes."]},{"glosses":["The orbits around Earth and other bodies with these qualities."],"tags":["broadly"]}],"upstream_line":4843910,"upstream_offset":11425535678,"upstream_record_sha256":"c69787dded1694ab3c47a8ed504c352d995b96d69c7e1253b1a91a38b4153d78","word":"Molniya orbit"}

{"forms":[{"form":"case of we","tags":["reflexive"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"pron","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"We should keep this for ourselves.","type":"example"},{"text":"Let's not beat ourselves up over this.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Us; the group including the speaker as the object of a verb or preposition when that group also is the subject."],"tags":["reflexive"]},{"examples":[{"text":"We did it ourselves.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Intensifies the subject as the group including the speaker, especially to indicate that no one else satisfies the predicate or needs to be included."],"tags":["emphatic"]},{"examples":[{"text":"Come on everyone! Let's get ourselves up and out of bed, please.","type":"example"},{"text":"Now then children, I'm not going to help you with the next problem. We should be able to work this one out ourselves.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Referring to everyone being addressed."],"tags":["colloquial"]},{"examples":[{"text":"Hello Mrs Miggins. Did we manage to wash ourselves this morning?","type":"example"},{"text":"Well done Timmy! Did we make that model ourselves?","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Referring to an individual person being addressed, especially a person in the speaker's care; also ourself."],"qualifier":"may seem patronising","tags":["colloquial"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/aʊəˈsɛlvz/","tags":["Received-Pronunciation","UK"]},{"ipa":"/ɑːˈsɛlvz/","tags":["Received-Pronunciation","UK"]},{"ipa":"/wɐˈsɛlz/","tags":["Northumbria","UK"]},{"ipa":"/wəˈsɛlz/","tags":["Northumbria","UK"]},{"ipa":"/aʊɚˈsɛlvz/","tags":["General-American"]},{"ipa":"/ɑɹˈsɛlvz/","tags":["General-American"]}],"synonyms":[{"tags":["dialectal","obsolete"],"word":"usself"},{"tags":["Jamaican","Scottish"],"word":"weself"}],"upstream_line":33052,"upstream_offset":225231939,"upstream_record_sha256":"395023cb8c9f740b254f0dfc09098a10ea5ec92889bf6b4b26b5d9ce55292a3f","word":"ourselves"}
